Decode cap160 means 40 requests / 160 EAGLE verification rows per DP rank.
Keep EAGLE steps3/draft4, the original FP8 operands and FP32 scales/partial
arithmetic. This build prepares one later two-node serving experiment; do
not add kernel-bench, profile or Firmus execution tags to this commit.

Replace the rejected T64 FA3 companion specializations with P128 non-TMA
stage2 split2/split3. Keep control96 and p128s2 and bound allocation/prepare
to batch32..48. Skip zero-weight partial O loads in combine: upstream leaves
empty split O unwritten, so 0*NaN was contaminating the output. All-empty
rows retain O=0/LSE=-inf. Poison partial O with NaN in the boundary/replay
fixture and require only -inf LSE for empty splits.

Raise the MegaMoE host SwapAB rollout bound144->192 and rename its series
entry. The existing independent-WG condition stays unchanged: EP4 Qwen
160/192 rows still use M64 and the opt-in six-stage independent queues.
Extend the dense 1d2d override bound128->192 and refresh its pinned patch
SHA. Raise baked Qwen top-k padding guards to192; the row-wise kernels
still process every row and retain their arithmetic.

Fix the image's independent-WG CPU compile helper to exclude that patch
from its control copy even when the serving config already enables it.
Then apply it once for the candidate and preserve control/prefill SASS checks.

Dense candidates retain the design's four shapes/settings per projection.
User-approved correction: QKV128x144 stage7 exceeds shared-memory capacity;
use stage5 (214016 bytes, maximum5). All other design stages fit unchanged.
Add MegaMoE192/hot-expert/mixed-boundary checks and balanced/skew160/192
bench cases, without launching a standalone bench.

Validation: CPU-only CUDA13.0.88 FA3 compile/link/operator registration in
image39932105942 succeeds in148.54s. Attention uses255 registers with zero
stack/spill, P128 shared memory109568B; SASS has cp.async and FP8 WGMMA,
no UTMALDG. The installed flash_ops SHA is unchanged. Host series apply and
full tvm_ffi_api.cpp syntax check pass. Actual host/layout checks pass480
conditions and64 dense config/boundary conditions. Overlay application,
Python AST, Ruff, shell syntax, line-continuation check and full pytest pass.
See cap160-work/notes.md and compile-results.md for exact evidence and limits.

No GPU job, Actions run, commit, push or branch operation was performed in
preparing this diff. GPU numerical gates, selector timing and official score
remain unmeasured. After this image build, pin the actual artifact in the
separate two-node Firmus serving commit.
